# Master Reference Database

_Generated: 2026-05-29_ · Mined from the `# REFERENCES` sections of all parsed papers in `raw/sources/`.

Deduplicated by normalized title (+ author-surname/year). `cited_by` lists the curated wiki sources (or raw folders) whose reference list contains the work; `cited_count` is its citation frequency **within this corpus** (a centrality/depth signal). Fields are faithful to the parses — missing fields are `n/a`, never guessed.

## Summary

- **Parses mined:** 41 `full.md` files → 39 distinct curated papers (2 duplicate parses collapsed).
- **Reference strings parsed:** 1929.
- **Unique references after dedup:** 1590.
- **Cited by >= 2 corpus papers:** 171 (foundational / depth signal).
- **Cited by >= 3 corpus papers:** 52.
